class="features-display-wrapper blueFullWidth contentWide fullWidth"><div class="FeaturesDisplay"><div class="content"><h2 class="displayTitle">The revolutionary features that set Glowforge apart.</h2><div class="selectedFeature"><div class="featureCard"><div class="image"><img alt="Features Display - Live Camera Preview" class="src" src="//images.ctfassets.net/ljtyf78xujn2/4NVZIKZzG7OUb1bBICREdo/2df55dd13b8c791240795f0e75dadb12/live-camera-preview.png?"/></div><div class="content"><h3 class="title">Live Camera Preview</h3><div class="markdown"><p>See your design take shape before you print. Onboard cameras show you a preview of your design on whatever material you choose. Place your design with your mouse or finger and position it exactly where you want.</p>
</div></div></div></div><div class="featuresGrid"><button class="secondary medium" disabled="" type="button"><span>Live Camera Preview</span></button><button class="secondary medium" type="button"><span>3D Autofocus</span></button><button class="secondary medium" type="button"><span>3D Hi-Res Engraving</span></button><button class="secondary medium" type="button"><span>Set up in minutes</span></button><button class="secondary medium" type="button"><span>Safe for schools</span></button><button class="secondary medium" type="button"><span>Laser Precision</span></button><button class="secondary medium" type="button"><span>Design with just a pen</span></button><button class="secondary medium" type="button"><span>Design Catalog</span></button><button class="secondary medium" type="button"><span>Compatible</span></button><button class="secondary medium" type="button"><span>Cloud Simplicity</span></button><button class="secondary medium" type="button"><span>Automatic Setup</span></button><button class="secondary medium" type="button"><span>Compact Filter Upgrade</span></button></div><div class="featureCards"><div class="HorizontalScroll fullWidth"><div class="scroll-content"><div class="featureCard"><div class="image"><img alt="Features Display - Live Camera Preview" class="src" src="//images.ctfassets.net/ljtyf78xujn2/4NVZIKZzG7OUb1bBICREdo/2df55dd13b8c791240795f0e75dadb12/live-camera-preview.png?"/></div><div class="content"><h3 class="title">Live Camera Preview</h3><div class="markdown"><p>See your design take shape before you print. Onboard cameras show you a preview of your design on whatever material you choose. Place your design with your mouse or finger and position it exactly where you want.</p>
</div></div></div><div class="featureCard"><div class="image"><img alt="Features Display - 3d AutoFocus" class="src" src="//images.ctfassets.net/ljtyf78xujn2/7kl0SwpFayAGLugtBjrbE/6ec22c6bbba61295da1b313c567e6ada/3d-autofocus.jpg?"/></div><div class="content"><h3 class="title">3D Autofocus</h3><div class="markdown"><p>Just like a camera, your Glowforge has a fully automatic focusing system to cut and engrave on materials both thick and thin. Before each print, the height is measured with exacting precision to be sure it prints perfectly, every time.</p>
</div></div></div><div class="featureCard"><div class="image"><img alt="3d-hi-res-engraving" class="src" src="//images.ctfassets.net/ljtyf78xujn2/3PhFisQr458OWtyCOIhnN6/378497269b2c698d628a7a696ce7a968/3d-hi-res-engraving.jpg?"/></div><div class="content"><h3 class="title">3D Hi-Res Engraving</h3><div class="markdown"><p>Engrave complex, three dimensional curves with 1,000 DPI resolution. To get perfect detail and sculpt with real depth, Glowforge can carve away material with multiple passes for truly detailed and impressive results.</p>
</div></div></div><div class="featureCard"><div class="image"><img alt="Set up in minutes" class="src" src="//images.ctfassets.net/ljtyf78xujn2/2N8g7ichWH4Q6cX9cYYets/6716462f055b3485569edf5ed004cf20/set-up-in-minutes.jpg?"/></div><div class="content"><h3 class="title">Set up in minutes</h3><div class="markdown"><p>The hardest part of setting up is deciding what to print first. Your Glowforge uses Wi-Fi and a regular outlet, and tutorials make the first prints a snap. Put your Glowforge near a window with the included exhaust hose or add a Glowforge Air Filter to print anywhere.</p>
</div></div></div><div class="featureCard"><div class="image"><img alt="Safe for schools" class="src" src="//images.ctfassets.net/ljtyf78xujn2/3HU28Vn1NxmFJBEQ81xbX8/1c848a5cfa259c73ac2ceb8da1278727/safe-for-schools.jpg?"/></div><div class="content"><h3 class="title">Safe for schools</h3><div class="markdown"><p>Under FDA regulations, the Glowforge Basic and Glowforge Plus are categorized as Class 1 lasers, like a DVD player, so no special precautions are required to use them. The Pro model is a Class 4 device like other lasers, and requires additional precautions.</p>
</div></div></div><div class="featureCard"><div class="image"><img alt="Laser Precision" class="src" src="//images.ctfassets.net/ljtyf78xujn2/7JEoYDqLS3VdIRLmhtZbT4/c5421f94cfd7ad3defd53e2cd7ab530b/laser-precision.jpg?"/></div><div class="content"><h3 class="title">Laser Precision</h3><div class="markdown"><p>Your Glowforge motors can control motion to a thousandth of an inch, and the laser can focus down to the width of a human hair. Combined, they can create prints with exquisite detail and precision.</p>
</div></div></div><div class="featureCard"><div class="image"><img alt="Design With Just A Pen" class="src" src="//images.ctfassets.net/ljtyf78xujn2/4TCqfPy6LL3WzHtNV4tGMP/4c0ad9e2ae0b4fbcfc1bd419b1adf31e/design-with-a-pen.jpg?"/></div><div class="content"><h3 class="title">Design with just a pen</h3><div class="markdown"><p>Just use a pen to draw directly onto your materials, and watch your designs transform. From a doodle to a masterpiece, onboard cameras scan and turn your drawing into a real product. Resize, copy, or personalize it further – you’re just one button away from a beautiful print.</p>
</div></div></div><div class="featureCard"><div class="image"><img alt="Design Catalog" class="src" src="//images.ctfassets.net/ljtyf78xujn2/ssIndRuU2t45Uq6KLbyYh/14528b3b7533810a52f139aa34a0ea27/design-catalog.png?"/></div><div class="content"><h3 class="title">Design Catalog</h3><div class="markdown"><p>When you don’t want to start from scratch, browse, buy, and print from the Glowforge catalog. Choose from dozens of ready-to-print designs that you can personalize, like jewelry, the perfect shoulder bag, or fun wall art. No design experience needed.</p>
</div></div></div><div class="featureCard"><div class="image"><img alt="Compatible" class="src" src="//images.ctfassets.net/ljtyf78xujn2/602ExRXupM9sjx9Bf9xjKD/bc1f6631ee051d4a2936a5ce2c694de3/Compatible.png?"/></div><div class="content"><h3 class="title">Compatible</h3><div class="markdown"><p>Use the format and system that’s best for you. You can work from your Mac, iPad, iPhone, Android, and Windows or Linux computer. Design with Inkscape, Adobe Photoshop and Illustrator, GIMP, Fusion 360, Corel Draw, and more – even PowerPoint or Google Docs! Compatible with JPG, PDF, PNG, and SVG.</p>
</div></div></div><div class="featureCard"><div class="image"><img alt="Cloud Simplicity" class="src" src="//images.ctfassets.net/ljtyf78xujn2/1zRT1xI7CxvBsFONq5rIqW/e2f0ba48d28727b9aa88b19a92343dfc/cloud-simplicity.jpg?"/></div><div class="content"><h3 class="title">Cloud Simplicity</h3><div class="markdown"><p>Your Glowforge runs from a web browser, which means there’s no software to install - ever. Drag and drop your designs right onto your material. Upgrades are instant and seamless.</p>
</div></div></div><div class="featureCard"><div class="image"><img alt="Automatic Setup" class="src" src="//images.ctfassets.net/ljtyf78xujn2/7CKCURBhllAIQfWtFdYKgs/b1c1b31da9d5d54cbbbd0a2e8c145d56/PastedGraphic-6__1_.png?"/></div><div class="content"><h3 class="title">Automatic Setup</h3><div class="markdown"><p>Whether you use a thick natural leather or beautiful maple, Proofgrade™ brand materials are automatically recognized by your device. Material settings are instantly configured so you can focus on what matters: your project.</p>
</div></div></div><div class="featureCard"><div class="image"><img alt="Final-Design-Compact-Filter-Staged" class="src" src="//images.ctfassets.net/ljtyf78xujn2/66xjEEx3NDm0rhhJYyoxkW/1a33e764b3c330787aae5c8d42863ebf/Final-Design-Compact-Filter-Staged.jpg?"/></div><div class="content"><h3 class="title">Compact Filter Upgrade</h3><div class="markdown"><p>The unique Compact Filter eliminates the need for outside ventilation. It sits on the ground near your Glowforge and cleans the air coming out of your Glowforge, allowing you to operate it anywhere in your home, school, or office! Alternately, you can use your Glowforge near a window with the included duct.</p>
</div></div></div></div><div class="arrow back"><span class="defaultWrapper"><svg class="icon chevron-light default hasClick" viewbox="0 0 45.9 87"><path d="M44.7 41.5L4 .8S2.2-1 .8.8.4 4 .4 4l39.9 39.8L.5 83s-1.4 1.8.5 3.2c1.4 1.4 3.2.5 3.2.5L44.9 46s1-1 1-2.2c-.2-1.5-1.2-2.3-1.2-2.3z"></path></svg></span></div><div class="arrow forward"><span class="defaultWrapper"><svg class="icon chevron-light default hasClick" viewbox="0 0 45.9 87"><path d="M44.7 41.5L4 .8S2.2-1 .8.8.4 4 .4 4l39.9 39.8L.5 83s-1.4 1.8.5 3.2c1.4 1.4 3.2.5 3.2.5L44.9 46s1-1 1-2.2c-.2-1.5-1.2-2.3-1.2-2.3z"></path></svg></span></div></div><span class="seeMore">← SWIPE TO SEE MORE →</span></div></div></div></div><div class="undefined-wrapper contentWide fullWidth"><div class="TextAndImageSection-beta rightImageCard"><div class="header inlineTop"><h2 class="title">Need something outstanding, fast?</h2></div><div class="contentWrapper rightImageCard"><div class="imageContainer half"><img alt="" class="image" src="//images.ctfassets.net/ljtyf78xujn2/4bYQCtNJWEtVtTI8t6DnVm/6a943cc96fd59248919157c885d58a5d/ipad_catalog_screenshot.png"/></div><div class="textContainer half"><div class="header inline"><h2 class="title">Need something outstanding, fast?</h2></div><div class="textContent"><div class="markdown paragraph"><style>
